Box kernels overwrite any mode kwarg with linear_interp. CustomKernel accepts only list/ndarray (not tuple), odd axes, float64. Model*DKernel set no _default_size.

angle_lextab.py[FB1T]: F:Auto-generated PLY lexer table caching the token regexes for angle string parsing. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/angles/formats.py | A:- | S:Generated; don't edit; rebuild if units change.
angle_parsetab.py[FB1T]: F:Auto-generated PLY LALR parse table for the angle grammar defined in formats.py. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/angles/formats.py | A:- | S:Generated; stale if grammar changes; rebuild it.
core.py[DB7L]: F:Defines Angle, Latitude and Longitude quantity classes with flexible string parsing, sexagesimal formatting, wrapping and bounds checks. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/angles/formats.py | A:Angle, Latitude, Longitude | S:Tuple input to Angle raises TypeError. Longitude wraps in place on set/setitem; Latitude validates on create/setitem. Their ufuncs return plain Angle. Unit hour is converted to hourangle.
errors.py[CB3S]: F:Defines range error exceptions and warnings for out-of-range hour, minute and second angle components. | R:- | A:RangeError, BoundsError, IllegalHourError, IllegalMinuteError, IllegalSecondError, IllegalHourWarning | S:Ctor args; warn(instance); value 60 only warns.
formats.py[CB5L]: F:Implements the PLY-based angle string parser and the sexagesimal conversion and range-check helpers used by Angle. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/angles/errors.py,code:astropy/coordinates/angles/angle_lextab.py,code:astropy/coordinates/angles/angle_parsetab.py | A:parse_angle | S:Rule order matters; grammar edits need regenerated lextab/parsetab. Parser thread-local. Hour 24, min/sec 60 only warn. Seconds carry at 60-10**-precision (8 if None); hidden fields round at 30.
utils.py[KB4M]: F:Provides spherical geometry helpers: angular separation, position angle, offset_by and uniform or golden-spiral point generators. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/angles/core.py | A:angular_separation, position_angle, offset_by, golden_spiral_grid, uniform_spherical_random_surface, uniform_spherical_random_volume | S:Plain floats are radians. Random funcs use global np.random. offset_by: poles as limit, returns deg, lon [0,360).

===/root/aoci-eval/repos-r5/astropy/astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/===
__init__.py[DB5M]: F:Imports all built-in coordinate frames and transform modules to register them in the graph and builds the transform-graph docstring. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/altaz.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/cirs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py | A:make_transform_graph_docs | S:Transform modules are imported only for registration side effects; the lsr import must come last. The order of __all__ sets the documentation order; __doc__ is overridden.
altaz.py[DB5S]: F:Defines the AltAz horizontal coordinate frame with observer location, time and atmospheric refraction attributes. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/icrs_observed_transforms.py | A:AltAz | S:pressure defaults to 0 (no refraction; inaccurate below ~5 deg alt); temperature deg_C, humidity 0-1. secz=1/sin(alt) negative below horizon. Self-transform: icrs_observed_transforms.py.
baseradec.py[DB4T]: F:Defines the BaseRADecFrame base class mapping spherical lon/lat to RA/Dec names for equatorial frames. | R:- | A:BaseRADecFrame, doc_components | S:Default differential is SphericalCosLatDifferential: RA proper motion carries the cos(dec) factor (pm_ra_cosdec).
cirs.py[DB4T]: F:Defines the CIRS (Celestial Intermediate Reference System) frame with obstime and location attributes. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:CIRS | S:Self-transform is via ICRS in icrs_cirs transforms module, not here. Location defaults to Earth centre.
cirs_observed_transforms.py[KB5S]: F:Registers CIRS<->AltAz and CIRS<->HADec transforms using ERFA atioq/atoiq with an observer astrometry context. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/cirs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/altaz.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/hadec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/erfa_astrom.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:cirs_to_observed, observed_to_cirs | S:Input CIRS with different obstime/location is first moved to CIRS at the observed frame's. AltAz uses zenith distance (PIOVER2 - lat). Distance unchanged; unit-spherical stays unit-spherical.
ecliptic.py[DB5M]: F:Defines the ecliptic coordinate frame classes (geocentric, barycentric, heliocentric, mean, true, IAU76, custom obliquity) and their frame attributes. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/ecliptic_transforms.py | A:BaseEclipticFrame, GeocentricMeanEcliptic, BarycentricTrueEcliptic, HeliocentricEclipticIAU76, CustomBarycentricEcliptic | S:Per-class attributes: obstime only on Geocentric/Heliocentric (not IAU76 or Barycentric); IAU76 has no equinox. CustomBarycentricEcliptic has only obliquity (default 84381.448 arcsec).
ecliptic_transforms.py[KB5M]: F:Registers transforms between ICRS/GCRS and all ecliptic frames, with mean, true and obliquity-only rotation matrices plus sun/barycenter offsets. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/ecliptic.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/icrs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/gcrs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:- | S:Heliocentric raises UnitsError without length units. IAU76 obliquity uses obl80 at J2000, not the equinox. Mean uses ecm06, true adds nutation. Self-transforms via _add_merged_transform.
equatorial.py[DB4S]: F:Defines the TETE (true equator, true equinox) and TEME equatorial frame classes. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:TETE, TEME | S:TEME: Cartesian default representation, obstime (no default). TETE: obstime, location (default EARTH_CENTER).
fk4.py[DB4M]: F:Defines FK4 and FK4NoETerms frames, the E-terms of aberration handling, Newcomb precession and their conversions. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/earth_orientation.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:FK4, FK4NoETerms, fk4_e_terms | S:FK4→FK4 goes via FK4NoETerms. E-terms solved by 10-iteration loop. obstime defaults to equinox. Distance renormalized.
fk4_fk5_transforms.py[KB4T]: F:Registers matrix transforms between FK4NoETerms and FK5 using Murray 1989 B1950-J2000 matrix and a time-dependent rotation correction. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/fk4.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/fk5.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:- | S:Dynamic: matrix depends on obstime (Julian centuries from 1950); array times get (1,1) appended to shape.
fk5.py[DB4T]: F:Defines the FK5 frame class with its equinox attribute, precession matrix helper and FK5 self-transform. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/earth_orientation.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:FK5 | S:Precession via _precession_matrix (IAU2006), also used by FK4 and Galactic transforms. Origin is barycenter.
galactic.py[DB4S]: F:Defines the Galactic frame class with l/b/u,v,w naming and the NGP and zero-point constants for FK4/FK5. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/fk4.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/fk5.py | A:Galactic | S:J2000 NGP and lon0 are derived values, not Reid and Brunthaler. Origin is the solar system barycenter.
galactic_transforms.py[KB3T]: F:Registers matrix transforms between Galactic and FK5/FK4NoETerms. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/galactic.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/fk4.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/fk5.py | A:- | S:Precess to J2000/B1950 first; inverse=transpose.
galactocentric.py[DB6L]: F:Defines the Galactocentric frame, its ScienceState default-parameter registry, and the affine ICRS<->Galactocentric transforms. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/icrs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/matrix_utilities.py | A:Galactocentric, galactocentric_frame_defaults, get_matrix_vectors | S:Defaults read at frame construction; explicit kwargs drop their reference entry. Transforms raise ConvertError for unit-spherical data or 2D velocities. Roll applied is get_roll0 minus roll.
gcrs.py[DB4S]: F:Defines GCRS and PrecessedGeocentric frame classes with obstime and observer geocentric position and velocity attributes. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:GCRS, PrecessedGeocentric | S:GCRS self-transform lives in ICRS-CIRS transforms module. PrecessedGeocentric differs from GCRS at J2000 equinox.
hadec.py[DB4S]: F:Defines the HADec hour-angle/declination frame with observer location, obstime and refraction attributes. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/altaz.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/cirs_observed_transforms.py | A:HADec | S:Wrap angle forced to 180 deg in __init__ and represent_as. HA negative east. Pressure 0 disables refraction.
hcrs.py[DB3T]: F:Defines the HCRS heliocentric frame, ICRS-aligned with Sun-centred origin and an obstime attribute. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:HCRS | S:No aberration; see icrs_cirs_transforms.py
icrs.py[DB4T]: F:Defines the ICRS frame class, the barycentric inertial RA/Dec reference frame used as the hub for transforms. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py | A:ICRS | S:Has no frame attributes, so it is a hub for other frames' transforms; they are defined in other modules.
icrs_cirs_transforms.py[KB6M]: F:Registers ICRS<->CIRS, ICRS<->GCRS, GCRS->HCRS and HCRS<->ICRS transforms using ERFA astrometry, plus loopback self-transforms. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/cirs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/gcrs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/hcrs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/icrs.py | A:- | S:With distance, parallax is applied first, in the reverse order on the way back, for round-tripping. HCRS<->ICRS raise UnitsError for unit-spherical data. Unit-spherical is detected by x.unit == u.one.
icrs_fk5_transforms.py[KB3T]: F:Registers ICRS<->FK5 dynamic matrix transforms using the USNO Circular 179 frame-bias matrix and precession. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/fk5.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/icrs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py | A:- | S:Equinox-dependent; bias matrix built at import.
icrs_observed_transforms.py[KB5S]: F:Registers direct ICRS<->AltAz and ICRS<->HADec transforms through ERFA, with parallax correction and optional refraction. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/altaz.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/hadec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/icrs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py,code:astropy/coordinates/erfa_astrom.py | A:- | S:AltAz uses zenith distance (lat = PIOVER2 - zen), erfa coord_type 'A'; HADec 'H'. UnitSpherical or dimensionless-x data skips parallax (observer eb in au). Loopbacks via _add_merged_transform.
intermediate_rotation_transforms.py[KB6M]: F:Registers rotation-only transforms among GCRS, CIRS, TETE, ITRS, TEME and PrecessedGeocentric, and defines the matrix helpers. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/cirs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/equatorial.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/gcrs.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/itrs.py | A:teme_to_itrs_mat, gcrs_to_cirs_mat, cirs_to_itrs_mat, tete_to_itrs_mat, gcrs_precession_mat, get_location_gcrs | S:Transforms first move to a GCRS at the target observer location and obstime. TEME uses GMST 1982 with no s'. Pass rbpn to tete_to_itrs_mat to avoid recomputing it.
itrs.py[DB4T]: F:Defines the ITRS frame, a Cartesian Earth-fixed frame with obstime and location, plus an earth_location property. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/utils.py,code:astropy/coordinates/earth.py | A:ITRS | S:earth_location adds self.location to the data, so a topocentric frame yields geocentric coordinates.
itrs_observed_transforms.py[KB5S]: F:Registers ITRS<->AltAz/HADec transforms by direct topocentric rotation with an ERFA-style refraction model. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/altaz.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/hadec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/itrs.py | A:itrs_to_altaz_mat, itrs_to_hadec_mat, altaz_to_hadec_mat, add_refraction, remove_refraction | S:Refraction is applied only when pressure > 0; HADec then goes via AltAz. Differing location or obstime goes through CIRS and changes aberration. Refraction is clamped by SELMIN and CELMIN.
lsr.py[DB4M]: F:Defines LSR, GalacticLSR, LSRK and LSRD velocity frames with affine velocity-offset transforms to ICRS/Galactic. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/baseradec.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/galactic.py | A:LSR, LSRD, LSRK, GalacticLSR | S:LSRK/LSRD offsets hard-coded (import cost). LSR/GalacticLSR v_bary is Galactic Cartesian; reverse negates.
skyoffset.py[DB5S]: F:Provides the SkyOffsetFrame factory and make_skyoffset_cls, which builds a cached per-origin-frame-class offset frame with transforms. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/attributes.py,code:astropy/coordinates/matrix_utilities.py | A:SkyOffsetFrame, make_skyoffset_cls | S:__new__ requires origin= and returns a dynamically made subclass, not SkyOffsetFrame. The origin must have data. Longitude wrap angle is forced to 180 deg. Pickling uses a custom reducer.
supergalactic.py[DB3T]: F:Defines the Supergalactic frame with sgl/sgb component names and the north supergalactic pole in Galactic coordinates. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/galactic.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/supergalactic_transforms.py | A:Supergalactic | S:_nsgp_gal is read by the transforms.
supergalactic_transforms.py[KB2T]: F:Registers static matrix transforms between Galactic and Supergalactic using the north supergalactic pole. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/supergalactic.py,code:astropy/coordinates/builtin_frames/galactic.py | A:- | S:-
utils.py[CB5L]: F:Shared constants and ERFA-based helpers (IERS polar motion, UT1-UTC, CIP, aticq/atciqz, Earth ephemeris) for the builtin coordinate frame transforms. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/earth.py,code:astropy/coordinates/representation/__init__.py,code:astropy/coordinates/solar_system.py | A:get_polar_motion, get_dut1utc, get_jd12, aticq, atciqz, prepare_earth_position_vel | S:Out-of-range IERS data only warns (default polar motion, UT1-UTC=0). solar_system imports deferred (circular imports). aticq/atciqz use a 90 arcsec light-deflection threshold; not exact ERFA copies.

===/root/aoci-eval/repos-r5/astropy/astropy/coordinates/representation/===
__init__.py[LB4T]: F:Package facade re-exporting representation and differential classes from its submodules, plus legacy registries kept for backwards compatibility. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/representation/base.py,code:astropy/coordinates/representation/cartesian.py | A:CartesianRepresentation, SphericalRepresentation, CylindricalRepresentation, BaseRepresentation, BaseDifferential, REPRESENTATION_CLASSES | S:Registry dicts and get_reprdiff_cls_hash are imported for compatibility but absent from __all__.
base.py[DB8L]: F:Defines base classes for 3D coordinate representations and differentials, with class registries, masking, arithmetic, differential handling and conversions. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/representation/spherical.py,code:astropy/coordinates/representation/cartesian.py | A:BaseRepresentation, BaseDifferential, BaseRepresentationOrDifferential, REPRESENTATION_CLASSES, DIFFERENTIAL_CLASSES, DUPLICATE_REPRESENTATIONS | S:Base* names are not registered; duplicate representation name warns and re-keys both by qualname, duplicate differential name raises. Registering clears get_reprdiff_cls_hash cache. Differentials need same shape and SI-unit key. Arithmetic/mean/sum/cross raise with differentials. represent_as rejects strings; same class plus differential_class mutates self._differentials in place. Masking one component masks all.
cartesian.py[DB6L]: F:Implements Cartesian representation and differential classes, including xyz array shortcuts, matrix transforms and ERFA-based norm, dot and cross. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/representation/base.py | A:CartesianRepresentation, CartesianDifferential | S:A single ndarray input is kept as _xyz and get_xyz may return a view, not a copy. xyz_axis is only valid with a single array. Arithmetic via _combine_operation raises if differentials are attached.
cylindrical.py[DB5S]: F:Implements cylindrical (rho, phi, z) representation and differential classes with Cartesian conversion and scaling. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/representation/base.py,code:astropy/coordinates/representation/cartesian.py,code:astropy/coordinates/representation/spherical.py | A:CylindricalRepresentation, CylindricalDifferential | S:Imports _spherical_op_funcs privately from spherical. represent_as has a direct PhysicsSpherical path skipping Cartesian. rho and z need equivalent units.
geodetic.py[DB4S]: F:Defines geodetic (WGS84, WGS72, GRS80) and bodycentric representations of lon, lat, height with ellipsoid-based Cartesian conversion. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/representation/base.py,code:astropy/coordinates/representation/cartesian.py | A:BaseGeodeticRepresentation, BaseBodycentricRepresentation, WGS84GeodeticRepresentation, WGS72GeodeticRepresentation, GRS80GeodeticRepresentation | S:Subclasses must set _ellipsoid or both _equatorial_radius and _flattening, else AttributeError.
spherical.py[DB8L]: F:Defines spherical, unit-spherical, physics-spherical and radial coordinate representations plus their differentials, with Cartesian conversion and transforms. | R:code:astropy/coordinates/representation/base.py,code:astropy/coordinates/representation/cartesian.py,code:astropy/coordinates/angles/__init__.py,code:astropy/coordinates/distances.py,code:astropy/coordinates/matrix_utilities.py | A:UnitSphericalRepresentation, SphericalRepresentation, PhysicsSphericalRepresentation, RadialRepresentation, SphericalDifferential, SphericalCosLatDifferential | S:Physics theta is colatitude (90 deg - lat); differentials flip sign. CosLat variants hold d_lon*cos(lat); conversion needs a base. Negative scale ops add 180 deg to lon. Negative distances rejected unless Distance(allow_negative=True). UnitSpherical.transform gives SphericalRepresentation unless O(3). Radial cannot go to Cartesian; transform needs a scaled identity matrix.
